# Exhaustive local agent-skill inventory

Generated 2026-07-30. This scans every accessible `SKILL.md` and `skill.md` beneath `/Users/admin`. It separates the local skill-router catalog from downloaded caches, upstream libraries, generated corpora, session copies, and historical snapshots.

## What “all” means here

- **68 manifest entries are in the local skill-router catalog.**
- **5,708 physical manifests** were found on disk.
- Those collapse to **1,558 distinct name+description records**, **1,045 names**, and **1,687 distinct file contents**.
- A file being present—or even appearing in the filesystem-generated router catalog—does not prove it is enabled, trusted, or callable in a particular session.
- macOS denied traversal into 130 protected operating-system/private-data directories. All user-controlled Codex, Claude, shared-agent, GIGA, project, and accessible cache roots were scanned.

## Machine-readable companions

- `inventory.json` — summary, skill-router catalog, every deduplicated record, and every physical manifest.
- `deduplicated-capabilities.csv` — one row per name+description record, with all paths.
- `physical-manifests.csv` — one row per on-disk manifest, including hash and source class.
